<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学图像中曲线解剖结构的分割因复杂拓扑、类别极不平衡、弱对比度和形态差异大而具有挑战性。现有深度学习方法多为特定解剖部位或模态定制，泛化能力有限；扩散模型虽表现好但采样计算成本高，难以用于高分辨率3D体积。

Method: 基于flow matching的3D分割模型，学习从简单源分布到目标血管表示的连续变换，实现复杂曲线几何的渐进式细化，同时保证高效推理。在三个不同解剖部位和模态的公开数据集上使用统一的架构和训练策略进行验证。

Result: 在门静脉、脑血管和冠状动脉三个公开数据集上，该方法优于通用分割方法和血管专用方法，尤其在薄分支保留和血管连续性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了3D曲线分割的state-of-the-art，并为高效、可泛化且临床适用的医学图像分析方法开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Segmentation of curvilinear anatomical structures in 3D medical images remains challenging due to complex topology, severe class imbalance, weak contrast, and large variations in structure morphology. While deep learning approaches for 3D curvilinear segmentation have been proposed, they are often tailored to specific anatomies or modalities, limiting generalization across clinical settings and leaving room for improvement. Recent generative models have shown the benefits of iterative prediction for structured segmentation tasks, yet diffusion-based methods suffer from computationally expensive sampling, hindering their use on high-resolution 3D volumes. We present 3D-CurvSegFlow, a flow matching-based model for 3D curvilinear structure segmentation. The model learns a continuous transformation from a simple source distribution to the target vascular representation, enabling progressive refinement of complex curvilinear geometries with efficient inference. We evaluate our method on Three public challenging datasets covering distinct anatomies and modalities: portal vein, cerebral vessel, and coronary arteries. Using a common architecture and training strategy across all tasks, our method outperforms general-purpose and vessel-specific approaches, with strong preservation of thin branches and vascular continuity. This work not only advances the state-of-the-art in 3D curvilinear segmentation but also opens new avenues for efficient, generalizable, and clinically applicable methods in medical image analysis.

</details>


### [40] [Open-Vocabulary 3D Object Detection with Co-Distillation Discovery and Dual Guidance Robust Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19973)
*Shangbo Yuan,Jie Xu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进开放词汇3D目标检测（3D-OVD）的框架，通过共蒸馏策略实现可靠的未知物体发现，并通过双引导学习方案增强模型训练的鲁棒性，在SUN RGB-D和ScanNetV2数据集上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D目标检测方法采用两阶段流程，但发现阶段常存在定位不准确和分类不匹配的问题，限制了后续模型训练的性能。

Method: 提出一个创新框架，包含两方面：(1) 共蒸馏策略，通过融合几何一致性、结构物体性和语义确定性的综合分数进行匈牙利匹配来蒸馏高质量的新物体；(2) 双引导学习方案，包括用于回归头的场景感知引导不确定性正则化，以及用于分类头的LLM引导层次对齐。

Result: 在SUN RGB-D和ScanNetV2数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比现有最先进方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过同时提升未知物体发现的可靠性和模型训练的鲁棒性，所提出的框架有效解决了开放词汇3D目标检测中两阶段流程的关键局限性。

Abstract: Recently, open-vocabulary 3D object detection (3D-OVD) has gained increasing attention for its ability to detect unseen objects in 3D scenes. Existing approaches typically adopt a two-stage pipeline that first discovers novel objects using foundation models and then trains a 3D-OVD model based on these discovered objects. Although effective, this pipeline often suffers from inaccurate localization and mismatched classification during the discovery stage, which subsequently limits the performance of the model training stage. To address these limitations, we advocate for improving both the reliability of novel object discovery and the robustness of model training, and propose an innovative framework. Specifically, for reliable discovery, our co-distillation strategy distills high-quality novel objects by applying Hungarian matching over a comprehensive score that incorporates geometric consistency, structural objectness, and semantic certainty. To enhance robust model training, we further propose a dual-guidance learning scheme, incorporating a scene-awareness-guided uncertainty regularization for the regression head and an LLM-guided hierarchical alignment for the classification head, effectively mitigating the negative effects of imprecise 3D bounding boxes and semantic ambiguity. Extensive experiments on SUN RGB-D and ScanNetV2 demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance gains over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/shangboyuan/Co-3DGT

</details>


### [41] [STEP: Score-Based Temporal Energy for Human Pose Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19987)
*Jakub Micorek,Mateusz Koziński,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP框架，利用PCA将姿态序列投影到白化主成分空间进行能量模型训练，避免原始坐标空间直接注入噪声导致的结构崩塌问题，并结合置信度权重机制提升骨骼视频异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于骨骼的视频异常检测方法通过去噪分数匹配训练能量模型，但直接在原始关节坐标中注入噪声会产生物理上不可能的姿态，且随着时间窗口扩大这种结构崩塌会严重恶化，限制了模型处理长序列的能力。

Method: STEP框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用主成分分析（PCA）将姿态序列投影到紧凑的白化PC空间中学习数据密度，使注入的噪声转化为物理上合理的姿态变化，从而支持更长视频序列的处理；(2) 基于姿态估计器的置信度得分实现序列级加权机制，缓解因遮挡或运动模糊导致的姿态估计误差。

Result: STEP在UBnormal数据集上以90.1%的AUROC超越此前骨骼状态最优方法12.2%，在ShanghaiTech基准上取得具有竞争力的结果，同时保持实时计算效率。

Conclusion: 在PC空间中训练能量模型有效解决了原始坐标空间中的结构崩塌问题，使模型能够处理更长序列并显著提升异常检测性能，且该方法简单轻量，具备实时推理能力。

Abstract: Skeleton-based Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) offers a robust, privacy-preserving solution for identifying abnormal behaviors. To model the distribution of normal static and moving poses, recent methods train Energy-Based Models (EBMs) via Denoising Score Matching (DSM). However, directly injecting noise, required for training, into raw joint coordinates creates physically impossible poses, and this structural collapse severely worsens as the temporal window expands. To address this, we introduce STEP, a simple framework that utilizes Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to project pose sequences into a compact, whitened PC-space. Learning the data density within this well-behaved PC-space ensures that the injected noise translates into physically plausible variations, which allows the model to process longer video sequences without the performance collapse of raw coordinate baselines. Additionally, to mitigate inherent pose estimation inaccuracies arising from occlusions or motion blur, we integrate a sequence-level weighting mechanism based on the estimator's confidence scores. Operating at real-time computational efficiency, our simple and lightweight framework outperforms the previous skeleton-based state-of-the-art by 12.2% (90.1% AUROC) on the challenging UBnormal dataset and achieves highly competitive results by improving on the ShanghaiTech benchmark.

</details>


### [42] [Point-Based 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views under Known Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20000)
*Magnus Kaufmann Gjerde,Joakim Bruslund Haurum,Jeppe Revall Frisvad,Markus Worchel,J. Andreas Bærentzen,Thomas B. Moeslund*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于不透明性beta surfels的可微分点渲染方法，结合显式伴随光传输公式，在稀疏视角3D重建中仅使用极少量图元即可获得比最强点云基线低28.5%的Chamfer距离，展示了传输驱动优化在受控直接光照条件下恢复紧凑表面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏视角3D重建方法要么依赖神经隐式表面，要么使用密集点表示（如高斯溅射），通常需要数十万图元。作者希望探索一种更紧凑的表面感知表示，通过物理光传输约束来引导重建，从而减少图元数量并提高几何精度。

Method: 提出基于不透明性（opacity-bearing）beta surfels的可微分点渲染方法。采用显式伴随（adjoint）光传输公式提供曲面几何和外观参数的梯度，使基于物理的光传输能够约束重建过程，实现surfel参数的端到端优化。

Result: 在五个合成物体、十个视角的重建实验中，方法在所有对比基线中取得最低的平均对称Chamfer距离，相比最强点云基线降低28.5%的Chamfer距离，平均仅使用267个surfels，比相关方法少约161个图元。方向性Chamfer结果也显示了更高的精度和竞争力的完整性。

Conclusion: 在受控直接光照条件下，紧凑的beta surfels结合基于传输的优化能够无需数十万图元即可恢复高质量表面，验证了物理光传输约束对紧凑表示的引导有效性。

Abstract: Sparse view 3D reconstruction is commonly addressed with neural implicit surfaces or dense point-based representations such as Gaussian splatting. Surface-aware splatting methods improve extracted geometry through oriented primitives and regularization, while RadiosityGS incorporates differentiable light transport through a radiosity inspired finite-element surfel formulation. We propose a differentiable point rendering method based on opacity-bearing beta surfels. An opacity explicit adjoint light transport formulation provides gradients for surfel geometry and appearance parameters, allowing physically based light transport to constrain reconstruction.
  Across five synthetic objects reconstructed from ten posed views, our method achieves the lowest mean symmetric Chamfer distance among the evaluated baselines and reduces mean Chamfer distance by 28.5% relative to the strongest point-based baseline while using only 267 surfels on average, approximately ~161 fewer primitives. Directional Chamfer results further show improved accuracy and competitive completion relative to related point-based methods. These results show that, in the controlled direct illumination setting, compact beta surfels combined with transport-based optimization can recover surfaces without relying on the tens to hundreds of thousands of primitives used by the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [43] [From Street View Imagery to Street Quality Indicators: Vision Language Inference for the Suburban 15-minute City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20026)
*Joan Perez,Giovanni Fusco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用最新版SAGAI（基于视觉语言模型的开源工作流），利用Google街景图像对法国尼斯东北郊区的街道景观质量进行大规模评估，重点关注人行道、行人入口密度和植被等与步行友好环境相关的指标。结果显示高质量的街道景观仅存在于少数紧凑型开发区和传统郊区，而住宅山坡区域尤为缺乏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在15分钟城市和步行友好城市规划背景下，街道景观质量评估日益重要，但传统实地调查在广大的郊区和城郊区域耗时耗力，缺乏可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 采用最新版SAGAI开源工作流，利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）对数千个Google Street View街道级观测点进行自动分析，评估人行道存在性、行人入口密度和植被覆盖等指标。新版框架改进了图像采集、地理一致性视图生成、多VLM架构支持、基于共识的推断和集成分析环境。

Result: 研究生成的街道景观质量地图显示，理想的街道景观质量仅出现在少数紧凑型开发区和传统郊区（faubourgs），而住宅山坡区域的街道景观质量尤为不足。SAGAI工作流成功地在数千个观测点上进行了高效的大规模评估。

Conclusion: 当代视觉语言模型能够支持大范围郊区地带的城市诊断，而这类区域的实地调查成本将高得难以承受。该方法展示了VLM技术可扩展、灵活且可解释的城市公共空间质量评估能力，为循证规划提供了新的工具支撑。

Abstract: Streetscape quality has become a central concern in contemporary urban planning, particularly within the framework of the pedestrian-friendly 15-minute city, where walkability and public-space quality are increasingly recognized as key determinants of urban performance. However, assessing streetscape qualities across large suburban and peri-urban territories remains challenging due to the time and resource demands of conventional field surveys. This paper presents a planning-oriented assessment of streetscape qualities in the north-eastern periphery of Nice (France) using the latest release of SAGAI (Streetscape Analysis with Generative AI), an open-source workflow that leverages vision-language models (VLMs) for large-scale streetscape analysis from Google Street View imagery. The new release addresses limitations of the original framework through improved image acquisition, geographically consistent view generation, support for multiple VLM architectures, consensus-based inference, and an integrated analytical environment. The workflow is applied to several thousand street-level observations to evaluate qualities relevant to pedestrian-friendly urban environments: sidewalk presence, pedestrian entrance density, and vegetation. The resulting maps reveal that the desired streetscape qualities characterize only a fraction of today's suburban streetscapes, mainly in compact developments and traditional suburban faubourgs, while they are particularly lacking on residential hills. The analysis demonstrates the potential of contemporary VLMs to support urban diagnostics in extensive suburban territories where fieldwork would be prohibitively time-consuming. Beyond the case study, the paper illustrates how recent advances in vision-language models can contribute to evidence-based planning by enabling scalable, flexible, and interpretable assessments of urban public-space quality.

</details>


### [44] [Gravity-aware partially calibrated absolute pose estimation from affine- or rotation-covariant features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20056)
*Marcus Valtonen Örnhag,Alberto Jaenal,Stefan Adalbjörnsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用IMU重力向量和特征局部几何信息（如SIFT描述子中的局部几何）来联合估计绝对位姿和焦距的两种高效求解器（UP1PfAC和UP2PfORI），相比传统方法需要更少的对应点样本和更低的计算成本，在大型公开数据集上实现了快速且准确的位置与焦距估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统位姿估计方法未能利用特征描述子（如SIFT）中嵌入的局部几何信息，虽然近期工作已证明该信息在相对和绝对位姿估计中的优势，但将其应用于部分标定的绝对位姿估计（即同时估计位姿和焦距）仍是一个未探索的领域。

Method: 作者从IMU数据获取重力向量，结合特征诱导的局部几何信息推导出新的约束条件，用于联合估计绝对位姿和焦距。基于此约束构建了两个求解器：UP1PfAC（利用单个仿射对应点）和UP2PfORI（利用两个方向协变特征）。

Result: 在大型公开数据集上的评估表明，所提出的求解器相比现有最先进方法，只需更少的样本（1-2个对应点而非4个点对应），计算成本更低，同时实现了快速准确的定位和焦距估计。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了部分标定绝对位姿估计中利用局部几何信息的研究空白，所提出的方法在效率和准确性上均优于传统方法，尤其适合现代基于RANSAC的鲁棒估计框架中的实际应用。

Abstract: Inertial measurement units (IMUs) are now standard in most consumer devices, such as smartphones, drones, and extended reality (XR) headsets. By fusing visual and inertial data, localization systems gain significantly in speed and robustness compared to vision-only or IMU-only approaches. However, traditional pose estimation methods fail to utilize the local geometric information embedded in feature descriptors like SIFT. Recent work has proved the advantages of leveraging this information for relative and absolute pose estimation, but its application to partially calibrated absolute pose estimation remains unexplored. In this paper, we derive novel constraints for joint estimation of absolute pose and focal length, making use of a gravity vector obtained from IMU data and the feature-induced local geometry, which we use to construct two efficient solvers: UP1PfAC, that operates given a single affine correspondence and UP2PfORI, which requires two orientation-covariant features. Unlike traditional, semi-calibrated absolute pose methods requiring four point correspondences, our solvers benefit from fewer samples and lower computational cost, simplifying robust estimation in modern RANSAC-like frameworks. We evaluate the proposed solvers against the state-of-the-art on large-scale public datasets and demonstrate that our method achieves fast and accurate localization and focal length estimation.

</details>


### [45] [V-REX: Efficient Specialist VLM Training for Veterinary X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20069)
*Tim Elsner,Nicole McNally,Andre Dourson,Michael Fitzke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文展示了在兽医放射学领域，通过重新设计完整的VLM流程（从文本分词、预训练到接地和推理），仅用远少于通用基础模型的参数、数据和计算资源，从零训练的专用模型即可超越大型基础模型，并开发了首个能够为兽医X光片生成诊断报告的VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM训练成本高昂，而创建领域专家通常被认为需要微调越来越大的基础模型。作者认为这一假设在兽医放射学领域是错误的，目标是证明通过精心工程可以从零构建高效且优秀的专用模型。

Method: 重新思考整个VLM流水线，包括文本分词、预训练、接地（grounding）和推理环节，引入新的生成式预训练和接地策略，以提高训练效率、数据利用率和下游性能，且不依赖任何外部数据。

Result: 仅使用通用模型的一小部分参数、数据和计算资源，开发了首个能为兽医X光片生成诊断报告的VLM，在该任务上以显著优势超越了开放基础模型。

Conclusion: 精细的工程优化可以替代盲目扩大模型规模的思路，领域专属VLM并不一定需要依赖大规模通用基础模型的微调，小型专用模型可以取得更好的效果。

Abstract: While generalist VLMs are expensive to train, creating domain experts is widely assumed to require fine-tuning increasingly large foundation models. We show that, in veterinary radiology, this assumption is misguided. By rethinking the entire VLM pipeline - from text tokenisation and pre-training to grounding and inference - we demonstrate that careful engineering can yield models that outperform much larger foundation models from scratch, without relying on any other data. Our approach introduces new strategies for generative pre-training and grounding that improve training efficiency, increasing data utilisation and downstream performance. Using only a fraction of the parameters, data, and compute of contemporary generalist models, we develop the first VLM capable of generating diagnostic reports for veterinary radiographs, surpassing open foundation models on this task by significant margin.

</details>


### [46] [HandMvNet: Real-Time 3D Hand Pose Estimation Using Multi-View Cross-Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20093)
*Muhammad Asad Ali,Nadia Robertini,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandMvNet是一种实时的多视角3D手部姿态与形状估计方法，通过多视角注意力融合机制解决单目方法的尺度-深度模糊问题，无需相机参数输入，在保证精度的同时大幅降低推理时间，适用于实时应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目手部姿态估计方法存在尺度-深度模糊问题，导致绝对手部姿态和形状估计不准确；而现有的多视角方法虽然能缓解此问题，但通常推理速度慢且依赖相机参数输入，难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出多视角注意力融合机制，有效整合来自多个视角的特征；设计无需相机参数输入的网络架构，直接学习3D几何信息，从而实现快速且准确的绝对手部姿态与形状估计。

Result: 在公开数据集上的定性和定量评估中，HandMvNet在相同设置下优于先前方法，同时大幅缩短推理时间，达到实时性能，并与最先进方法取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: HandMvNet首次实现了实时的多视角3D手部运动与形状估计，兼顾精度与速度，无需相机参数输入，为实时手部交互应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this work, we present HandMvNet, one of the first real-time method designed to estimate 3D hand motion and shape from multi-view camera images. Unlike previous monocular approaches, which suffer from scale-depth ambiguities, our method ensures consistent and accurate absolute hand poses and shapes. This is achieved through a multi-view attention-fusion mechanism that effectively integrates features from multiple viewpoints. In contrast to previous multi-view methods, our approach eliminates the need for camera parameters as input to learn 3D geometry. HandMvNet also achieves a substantial reduction in inference time while delivering competitive results compared to the state-of-the-art methods, making it suitable for real-time applications. Evaluated on publicly available datasets, HandMvNet qualitatively and quantitatively outperforms previous methods under identical settings. Code is available at github.com/pyxploiter/handmvnet.

</details>


### [47] [Structured Affinity for Unsupervised Visual Class-Incremental Memory in Deep Artificial Immune Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20104)
*Siphesihle Sithungu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于结构化免疫亲和力的深度人工免疫网络（Deep AIN），通过保留响应图谱作为记忆表示，实现无需回放（replay-free）的视觉类增量学习。在多个数据集上验证了特征图谱Deep AIN优于标量绑定配置文件变体，并结合层间尺度校准进一步提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉人工免疫网络通常依赖扁平化向量亲和度，忽略了空间结构信息，且缺乏深度结构。本文旨在探索结构化、无梯度的免疫亲和力能否使Deep AIN成为无需回放的视觉类增量表示记忆学习器。

Method: 将视觉B细胞形式化为结构化模板（包括移位模板亲和度、ZNCC滤波器和特征图绑定配置文件）；将抗体库既作为记忆又作为表示诱导基；通过将绑定配置文件响应图传递给后续免疫层获得深度结构。同时引入自适应层间尺度校准。

Result: 在sklearn digits上，逻辑回归下游探针达到0.939最终平衡精度，1近邻达到0.902，初始类保留率为0.978；两层特征图谱Deep AIN经自适应尺度校准后达到0.978平衡精度；Fashion-MNIST达到0.814，KMNIST达到0.853。标量绑定配置文件变体表现不佳，证明响应图保留至关重要。

Conclusion: 结构化亲和度、响应图保留、自适应潜在坐标重组和层间尺度校准是无需回放的视觉免疫记忆的关键机制。Deep AIN可以在无回放、无标签驱动免疫更新、无需反向传播的条件下学习具有类别区分性的视觉记忆。

Abstract: Artificial immune networks (AINs) are naturally memory-forming systems, but conventional visual AINs often rely on flattened vector affinity that ignores spatial structure. This paper studies whether structured, gradient-free immune affinity can make Deep AINs viable as replay-free visual class-incremental representation-memory learners. Visual B-cells are formalized as structured templates, including shifted-template affinity, zero-normalized cross-correlation (ZNCC) filters, and feature-map binding profiles. A repertoire is treated both as memory and as a representation-inducing basis, while depth is obtained by passing binding-profile response maps to subsequent immune layers. The resulting Deep AIN exhibits adaptive latent coordinate reorganization: as new classes arrive, the binding-profile space evolves while retaining recoverable structure for earlier classes. Experiments on sklearn digits, MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and KMNIST show that preserving response maps is critical. Scalar binding-profile variants underperform, whereas feature-map Deep AINs learn class-discriminative visual memory without replay, label-driven immune updates, or backpropagation through the immune layers. On sklearn digits, downstream probes fitted on the learned binding profiles reach 0.939 final balanced accuracy with logistic regression and 0.902 with 1-nearest-neighbour after all ten classes are encountered, with initial-class retention of 0.978. Adaptive layer-wise scale calibration further improves the two-layer feature-map Deep AIN to 0.978 balanced accuracy. With the same calibration rule, Fashion-MNIST reaches 0.814 and KMNIST reaches 0.853. These probes are external validation tools, not components of the AIN. The results identify structured affinity, response-map preservation, adaptive latent reorganization, and layer-wise scale calibration as key mechanisms for replay-free visual immune memory.

</details>


### [48] [BeyondMasks: Evaluating Causal and Physical Consistency in Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20107)
*Yigit Ekin,Enes Sanli,Aykut Erdem,Erkut Erdem,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BeyondMasks基准，用于评估视频物体移除中的因果一致性，并引入CORE评估协议，揭示现有方法虽然有高掩膜区域保真度，但在移除阴影、反射等物理后效方面存在系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除评估只关注掩膜区域的局部修复质量，忽略了真实场景中物体移除是一种因果干预，需同时移除其引发的物理效应（如阴影、反射、光照变化等）。现有基准缺乏对齐的干净参考或限于简化合成环境，无法系统评估因果一致性。

Method: 构建BeyondMasks配对基准，包含时间对齐的合成与真实世界视频对及干净背景参考，覆盖多样的光度、几何、体积和动态交互，支持基于掩膜和指令驱动编辑。同时提出CORE评估协议，基于结构化视觉语言模型，联合衡量物体消失与物理后效一致性。

Result: 对最先进方法的基准测试显示，尽管掩膜区域保真度高，但在移除次级物理效应方面存在系统性失败，暴露了视觉合理性与因果正确性之间的差距。

Conclusion: BeyondMasks将视频物体移除重新定义为因果场景一致性而非局部重建问题，并提供了统一的评估框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have significantly improved visual realism in video object removal, yet evaluation protocols still focus on masked region fidelity, treating removal as local inpainting. In real scenes, object removal is a causal intervention: eliminating an object also requires removing its induced physical effects, such as shadows, reflections, illumination changes, translucency, and dynamic traces. Existing benchmarks lack aligned clean references or remain limited to simplified synthetic settings, preventing systematic evaluation of causal consistency. We introduce BeyondMasks, a paired benchmark for causally consistent video object removal, consisting of temporally aligned synthetic and real world video pairs with clean background references. The dataset spans diverse photometric, geometric, volumetric, and dynamic interactions, and supports both mask based and instruction driven editing. We further propose CORE, a structured vision language model based evaluation protocol that jointly measures object disappearance and after effect consistency, aligning more closely with human judgments than existing metrics. Benchmarking state of the art methods reveals systematic failures in removing secondary physical effects despite high masked region fidelity, exposing a gap between visual plausibility and causal correctness. BeyondMasks reframes video object removal as causal scene consistency rather than local reconstruction and provides a unified framework for its evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [ArmorOCR: Grounded Adversarial Visual Perception via Observation-Transferred Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20122)
*Linhan Cao,Siyuan Li,Jun Lan,Liangbo He,Guannan Li,Xiaolei Huang,Jun Jia,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvSpot，这是首个用于对抗性OCR评估的地面真值基准，包含390张图像和13种细粒度对抗OCR类型的区域级标注；同时提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架，通过OPSD和GRPO显著提升对抗性OCR感知能力，同时保持通用OCR能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型虽具备强大的OCR识别能力，但对人类可读而模型难以定位和识别的对抗性视觉文本仍然脆弱。现有OCR基准主要聚焦自然或文档风格文本，对抗性OCR评估在规模、任务覆盖度和区域感知评估方面存在明显不足。

Method: 提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)从特权变换观测中获取缺失的对抗性OCR感知能力；第二阶段通过Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)结合任务条件奖励，优化定位、识别、完整发现和视觉问答等地面OCR感知能力。

Result: 在AdvSpot及其他对抗性OCR基准和通用OCR基准上的实验表明，ArmorOCR在一致提升对抗性OCR感知能力的同时，保持了具有竞争力的通用OCR性能。

Conclusion: AdvSpot为对抗性OCR评估提供了首个地面感知基准，ArmorOCR框架有效解决了对抗性OCR感知的挑战，并为该领域未来研究奠定了基准和方法基础。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have demonstrated strong OCR recognition capabilities, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial visual text that is readable to humans but challenging for models to localize and recognize. Existing OCR benchmarks mainly focus on natural or document-style text, while adversarial OCR evaluations remain limited in scale, task coverage, or region-aware evaluation. In this paper, we formulate adversarial OCR as a \textbf{grounded OCR perception} task and introduce \textbf{AdvSpot}, the first benchmark for grounded adversarial OCR evaluation. AdvSpot comprises 390 images with region-level annotations, spanning 5 primary categories and 13 fine-grained adversarial OCR types. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{ArmorOCR}, a two-stage training framework for robust adversarial OCR perception. ArmorOCR first acquires missing adversarial OCR perception from privileged transformed observations through On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), and then refines grounded OCR perception through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-conditioned rewards for localization, recognition, full spotting, and visual question answering (VQA). Experiments on our AdvSpot, other adversarial OCR benchmarks, and general OCR benchmarks demonstrate that ArmorOCR consistently improves adversarial OCR perception while preserving competitive general OCR capability.

</details>


### [50] [ID-VTG: Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20127)
*Minghang Zheng,Jingli Wei,Hongyi Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像消歧视频时间定位（ID-VTG）任务，利用多模态查询（参考图像+文本描述）精准定位视频片段，并提出基于双分支快慢架构的VGD-Agg框架，在构建的两个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时间定位方法在面对视觉相似实体的多个事件时，仅依赖文本查询难以区分，因为细粒度视觉属性难以用文字准确描述。因此需要引入参考图像来辅助消歧。

Method: 提出VGD-Agg框架，采用双分支快慢架构：快分支高效生成初步事件候选，慢分支进行视频帧与参考图像之间的细粒度帧级匹配。通过两个可学习token增强判别能力：Compare Token代表硬负样本以探测目标实例是否存在，Depress Value代表与文本无关的事件；被识别为缺乏目标实例的候选被推向Depress Value。

Result: 构建了两个基准数据集（IDVTG-Gym和IDVTG-InternVid），并在两个基准上均取得了最先进的性能，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 图像消歧可以有效解决仅靠文本无法区分视觉相似实体的问题，VGD-Agg框架通过可学习的消歧机制显著提升视频时间定位在细粒度场景下的准确性。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) faces significant challenges when natural language queries must distinguish between multiple events involving visually similar entities, particularly when relying on fine-grained visual attributes that are difficult to describe accurately in words alone. To address this, we introduce Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding (ID-VTG), a task that leverages multimodal queries combining a reference image and a text description to precisely localize segments where a specific instance performs a described action. To facilitate research, we construct two benchmarks: IDVTG-Gym, focusing on fine-grained, compositionally ordered gymnastics actions with athletes in similar uniforms; and IDVTG-InternVid, an open-world dataset featuring diverse entities (e.g., humans, animals, fictional characters) and significant temporal distractors. Methodologically, we propose the Visually-Guided Disambiguation Aggregation (VGD-Agg) framework based on a dual-branch fast-slow architecture. The fast branch efficiently generates preliminary event proposals, while the slow branch performs fine-grained frame-level matching between video frames and the reference image. We enhance discriminability via two learnable tokens: a Compare Token, which represents hard negatives to probe for the presence of the target instance (as referred to by the query image), and a Depress Value, which represents text-irrelevant events. Proposals that the Compare Token identifies as lacking the target instance are pushed toward the Depress Value, thus easing disambiguation via the text query. Extensive experiments validate our approach, which achieves state-of-the-art results on the proposed benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/ID-VTG.

</details>


### [51] [Feature Evolution and Migration during Vision Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20134)
*Joonas Järve,Halil Ibrahim Aysel,Tarun Khajuria,Meelis Kull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的视角来可视化Vision Transformer（ViT）的训练过程，通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从CLS token表示中提取稀疏特征，并沿着网络深度（层）和训练时间（epoch）两个维度分析特征演化，揭示了训练过程中特征迁移（feature migration）的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注表示层面的相似性度量，难以捕捉训练过程中特征层面的动态变化，缺乏对ViT内部特征如何随训练演化的深入理解。

Method: 使用Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs)从CLS-token表示中提取候选稀疏特征，比较不同epoch-layer对之间的特征激活剖面（activation profiles），从而研究特征演化动态；并据此定义和量化特征迁移（feature migration）现象。

Result: 实验表明：特征迁移集中在训练早期；相比向深层迁移，特征更多地向浅层迁移；随着特征组织趋于稳定，迁移频率下降。同时发现深层网络比浅层网络更早且更强烈地趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为理解ViT学习和特征演化过程的有效工具，为深度网络的可解释性研究提供了新的分析维度。

Abstract: We present a novel view on feature evolution in Vision Transformers (ViTs) by visualizing the training process over two dimensions -- network depth (layer) and training time (epochs). We employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract candidate sparse features from CLS-token representations and compare their activation profiles across epoch--layer pairs. This allows us to study feature-level dynamics that are not directly visible from representation-level similarity measures. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this framework of feature evolution allows us to describe feature migration, the change in the layer where a feature is most detectable during training. Our experiments show that migration is concentrated early in training, occurs more often toward earlier layers than toward deeper layers, and declines as feature organization stabilizes. We further find that deeper layers stabilize earlier and more strongly than shallow layers. The results show that our approach can be employed as a tool for understanding how ViTs learn and evolve.

</details>


### [52] [DPC-Net: Dual-Prior Collaborative Network for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20141)
*Zhaokun He,Kangbiao Shi,Axi Niu,Jian Jin,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPC-Net，一种双先验协作网络，通过联合利用退化-语义耦合先验和低级视觉先验，在全能图像恢复任务中实现高质量重建，克服现有方法忽视语义和缺乏低级视觉先验的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AiOIR方法在退化建模中忽视图像语义，且重建阶段缺乏低级视觉先验，导致结构失真和语义不一致问题。

Method: 提出双先验协作网络（DPC-Net），包含：(1) 退化感知网络（DAN）提取退化-语义耦合特征，由视觉语言模型（VLM）监督特征分布；(2) 退化-语义调制模块（DSMM）实现耦合表示传播；(3) 双先验协作重建模块（DPCR）整合低级视觉先验与耦合先验指导图像重建。

Result: 在多个恢复基准上，DPC-Net相比最先进的AiOIR方法取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过同时引入语义级和低级视觉双先验，DPC-Net有效提升了全能图像恢复的质量和保真度。

Abstract: All-in-One Image Restoration (AiOIR) aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified model. However, existing methods often overlook image semantics in degradation modeling and lack low-level visual priors during reconstruction, leading to structural distortions and semantic inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a novel Dual-Prior Collaborative Network (DPC-Net), which achieves high-quality restoration by jointly exploiting degradation-semantic coupled priors and low-level visual priors. Specifically, degraded images are fed into a Degradation-Aware Network (DAN) to extract degradation-semantic coupled features. To this end, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervises DAN by constraining its features distribution, introducing image semantics into the encoding of degradation patterns. A Degradation-Semantic Modulation Module (DSMM) further translates this guidance into degradation-semantic coupling and propagates coupled representations to the decoder. During decoding, knowledge bases provide low-level visual priors, and the Dual-Prior Collaborative Reconstruction Module (DPCR) integrates dual-prior information to guide degradation removal while preserving structure and semantics, producing high-fidelity restored images. Extensive experiments on multiple restoration benchmarks demonstrate that DPC-Net achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art AiOIR methods.

</details>


### [53] [PelviNeXt: A Modality-Agnostic Hybrid Network for Pelvic Imaging in Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20144)
*Siam Tahsin Bhuiyan,Rashedur Rahman,Sefatul Wasi,Halima Khatun,Ashraful Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman,Saadia Binte Alam,M Ashraful Amin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PelviNeXt，一种模态无关的混合架构，用于盆腔超声和X射线图像分析。作者在PCOSGen数据集上发现了大量重复数据污染，通过感知哈希去重并发布修正版数据集，同时在骨盆骨折X射线数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性健康在医学影像研究中资源严重不足，多囊卵巢综合征（PCOS）和骨盆骨折等盆腔疾病缺乏公开且标注完善的基准数据集，阻碍了该领域研究进展。

Method: 提出模块无关的混合架构PelviNeXt，包含密集卷积特征提取器、层级通道-空间注意力（H-CBAM）、多尺度融合模块（MSFM）和talking-heads多头自注意力（TH-MHSA），可直接应用于盆腔超声和X射线输入；同时通过感知哈希审计并去重PCOSGen数据集。

Result: 在PCOSGen数据集上发现大量重复污染并发布去重版本；在骨盆骨折X射线数据集PXR150上，PelviNeXt在准确率、召回率、特异性和AUROC上均超越此前SOTA结果；消融实验验证了各组件均对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 单一架构无需任务特定修改即可作为盆腔影像（跨模态、数据稀缺、女性健康欠研究领域）的可靠基础模型，同时强调数据集完整性审计的重要性。

Abstract: Women's health remains substantially under-resourced in medical imaging research, with pelvic pathologies such as polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) and pelvic fracture both suffering from a scarcity of public, well-annotated benchmark data despite their clinical importance. We introduce PelviNeXt, a modality-agnostic hybrid architecture combining a dense convolutional feature extractor, hierarchical channel-spatial attention (H-CBAM), a multi-scale fusion module (MSFM), and talking-heads multi-head self-attention (TH-MHSA), applied without modification to both pelvic ultrasound and X-ray inputs. While benchmarking PelviNeXt on PCOSGen, the only gynaecologist-annotated public PCOS ultrasound dataset, we identified extensive exact and near-duplicate contamination within and across the dataset. We audit this contamination via perceptual hashing, publicly release a deduplicated version of the dataset, and establish the first integrity-audited evaluation protocol and baseline for PCOSGen under 5-fold cross-validation. On the only publicly available pelvic fracture X-ray dataset (PXR150), PelviNeXt exceeds previously reported state-of-the-art results across accuracy, recall, specificity, and AUROC. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component contributes to performance on both tasks. Our results demonstrate that a single architecture, applied without task-specific modification, can serve as a reliable foundation for pelvic imaging across modalities in data-scarce, under-researched areas of women's health.

</details>


### [54] [Artificial Intelligence for Workflow Analysis in Colorectal Surgery: A Multicentric, Cross-Procedural Development and Generalization Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20154)
*Pietro Mascagni,Julia Alekseenko,Pooja P Jain,Marta Goglia,Andrea Balla,Ludovica Baldari,Gianfranco Silecchia,Claudio Fiorillo,Vincenzo Tondolo,Salvador Morales-Conde,Luigi Boni,Sergio Alfieri,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Minimally invasive colorectal surgeries (MIS-CRS) are characterised by significant variability and inconsistent outcomes. ColoWorkflow, a tool for the video-based assessment (VBA) of MIS-CRS workflow, was recently validated. However, manual VBA is time-consuming, limiting implementation. This study presents AI-ColoWorkflow, a deep learning model for automated surgical workflow analysis across MIS-CRS. Operative videos of MIS-CRS were collected from 4 centres and a publicly available dataset. Phases and steps were manually annotated according to ColoWorkflow. A deep learning model combining a fine-tuned DINOv3 vision transformer for per-frame visual feature extraction with a hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network was jointly optimized for phase and step recognition. The model trained on pooled multicentric data, namely AI-ColoWorkflow was compared against centre-specific and procedure-specific models on a held-out test set. The following metrics were used for evaluation: macro F1 score, balanced accuracy, precision, and recall. AI-ColoWorkflow achieved a macro F1 of 73.01% $\pm$ 10.27 (balanced accuracy 73.43%) for phase recognition and 39.82% $\pm$ 7.06 (balanced accuracy 38.65%) for step recognition. The global model outperformed centre- and procedure-specific models in most experiments except procedure-specific step recognition. In the generalization analysis, mean F1 was 48.42% for phase recognition. AI-ColoWorkflow can reliably recognize MIS-CRS phases. A single model trained on pooled, multicentric, multi-procedural data generalises at least as well as and often better than centre- or procedure-specific models for phase recognition in MIS-CRS, while procedure-specific step models retain advantages for certain procedure types, motivating hybrid training strategies for future surgical AI development.

</details>


### [55] [G3Ego: Gaze-Guided Graphs for Egocentric Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20157)
*Marko Haralović,Akash Ramakrishnan,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G3Ego，一个基于图的自我中心动作理解框架，利用注视（gaze）作为结构线索来识别场景中与动作相关的实体。G3Ego从稀疏采样帧中构建动作场景图，并通过注视信息剪枝无关实体，最终进行动作识别和预测。在EGTEA Gaze+和MECCANO数据集上取得了与视频方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心动作理解通常依赖在大量外中心数据上预训练的大规模视频模型，但第一人称动作往往只涉及少数手-物交互和少量相关实体。现有方法将注视仅作为辅助模态或注意力信号，未能充分利用其结构价值。

Method: G3Ego从稀疏采样帧中，利用视觉-语言描述、接地物体和手部线索构建动作场景图，然后使用穿戴者注视信息剪枝无关实体，得到的图嵌入通过时间聚合用于动作识别和预测。注视被直接融入图构建过程，而非作为辅助信号。

Result: 在EGTEA Gaze+和MECCANO数据集上，G3Ego与基于视频的方法相比取得了有竞争力的性能，并在类别不平衡评估下持续提升Macro-F1，同时避免了对计算昂贵的视频预训练的依赖。

Conclusion: 注视引导的图表示在自我中心动作理解中是有效的，既提供了高效且可解释的表示，又聚焦于动作相关的交互，避免了大规模视频预训练的计算开销。

Abstract: Egocentric action understanding is often addressed using large video models pretrained on extensive exocentric datasets. However, many first-person actions depend on a small number of hand-object interactions involving only a few relevant entities.
  We propose G3Ego, a graph-based framework for egocentric action understanding that uses gaze as a structural cue to identify action-relevant entities in the scene. From sparsely sampled frames, G3Ego constructs action scene graphs from vision-language descriptions, grounded objects, and hand cues, and then prunes irrelevant entities using the camera wearer's gaze.
  The resulting graph embeddings are temporally aggregated for action recognition and anticipation. Unlike prior work that uses gaze primarily as an auxiliary modality or attention signal, G3Ego incorporates gaze directly into graph construction, producing efficient and interpretable representations focused on action-relevant interactions.
  Experiments on EGTEA Gaze+ and MECCANO show that G3Ego achieves competitive performance compared with video-based approaches and consistently improves Macro-F1 under class-imbalanced evaluation, while avoiding reliance on computationally expensive video pretraining. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of gaze-guided graph representations for egocentric action understanding.

</details>


### [56] [RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20208)
*Shaoxuan Wang,Guangting Zheng,Rui Huang,Zhipeng Tang,Sha Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMAN-Flow是一个离线强化学习框架，通过采样无关的advantage-weighted似然目标和无损蒸馏为单步动作生成器，解决了自回归归一化流（AR-NF）策略在离线机器人操作中的采样瓶颈问题，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习利用已有数据训练机器人策略无需环境交互，但主流扩散/流匹配策略缺乏可处理的似然函数，限制了基于似然的离线RL后训练应用。AR-NF虽同时具备强大的动作建模能力和精确似然评估，但其顺序采样过程在策略优化和部署阶段带来了显著的采样开销。

Method: RoMAN-Flow提出两个关键策略：1）在策略优化阶段采用采样无关的advantage-weighted似然目标，直接为离线数据中高优势动作分配更高似然而无需从自回归策略中采样；2）在部署阶段将优化后的自回归策略蒸馏为单步动作生成器，实现低延迟动作预测。

Result: 在多个模拟操作基准和真实机器人平台上实验表明，RoMAN-Flow达到了具有竞争力的策略性能，同时显著降低了推理延迟。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RoMAN-Flow有效解决了AR-NF策略在离线RL后训练中的采样瓶颈，使AR-NF策略在实际机器人操作任务中变得可行和实用，实现了性能与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning improves robotic policies using previously collected data without further environment interaction. Yet prevalent diffusion- and flow-matching robot policies lack tractable likelihoods, limiting their use in likelihood-based offline RL post-training. AR-NFs offer both expressive action modeling and exact likelihood evaluation, but their sequential sampling incurs substantial sampling overhead during policy optimization and deployment. We present RoMAN-Flow (Robotic Manipulation with Autoregressive Normalizing Flows), an offline reinforcement learning framework that makes AR-NF policies practical for robotic manipulation by addressing this sampling bottleneck in both stages. During policy optimization, RoMAN-Flow employs a sampling-free, advantage-weighted likelihood objective that assigns higher likelihood to high-advantage actions from the offline dataset without sampling from the autoregressive policy. For efficient deployment, it distills the optimized autoregressive policy into a one-step action generator, enabling low-latency action prediction. Experiments across multiple simulated manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that RoMAN-Flow achieves competitive policy performance while substantially reducing inference latency. Code is available at https://github.com/konnyaku28/RoMAN-Flow.

</details>


### [57] [Unwarping the Lens: A Physics-Grounded Approach to Video Glasses Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20212)
*Radim Spetlik,David Futschik,Radek Danecek,Feitong Tan,Ziqian Bai,Rohit Pandey,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从视频中高保真移除眼镜的迁移框架，利用商业生成模型（Nano Banana/Gemini 3 Pro Image）提取合成图像，经三级结构过滤后，结合物理透镜光学模拟进行训练，并将知识迁移到专用恢复架构JFSnet中，实现身份、表情、姿态保持下的高效眼镜移除（27.68 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼镜移除方法依赖大规模生成先验进行静态图像修复，缺乏结构约束，导致身份漂移（identity drift）问题；且眼镜造成的折射畸变和镜面反射使底层面部几何被遮蔽，高保真视频级眼镜移除至今仍具挑战。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 从商业级生成模型提取高保真合成人脸图像；(2) 通过三级结构过滤（结构/表情/姿态）对图像进行正则化；(3) 训练阶段引入基于物理的透镜光学模拟生成多样化配对数据。最终将先验知识迁移至专用恢复网络JFSnet，该网络融合DINOv2语义特征与卷积解码器，并利用平移等变约束增强时间一致性与高频细节保留。

Result: 在12163张FFHQ子集上实现高保真和结构准确，推理速度达27.68 FPS；在CelebV-Text视频序列的感知研究中，在眼部一致性、时间稳定性和整体修复质量上均优于扩散和GAN基线。

Conclusion: 通过"教师-学生"知识迁移思路，将生成模型的照片级多视角能力注入专用恢复架构，在不牺牲推理速度的前提下解决了眼镜移除中的身份漂移和时间不一致问题，验证了生成先验与物理仿真结合的有效性。

Abstract: High-fidelity removal of eyeglasses from video is a major challenge in facial attribute editing, as the underlying facial geometry is often obscured by complex refractive distortions and view-dependent specular reflections. While large-scale generative priors have shown promise in eye-glasses removal via static image inpainting, they often lack the structural constraints necessary to maintain identity, expression, and pose, leading to visible "identity drift" in both static images and dynamic sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel transfer framework that addresses the stochastic nature of generative priors. Our pipeline first extracts high-fidelity synthetic face images from a commercial-grade generative model (Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image), regularizes them via a three-stage structural filtering process to preserve identity, expression, and pose, and finally applies physically-based simulation of lens optics during training to provide diverse, paired data. This process transfers Nano Banana's photo-realistic, multi-view knowledge into a specialized restoration architecture, JFSnet (Joint Feature-Spatial network). JFSnet integrates DINOv2-based semantic features with a convolutional decoder for spatial reconstruction, leveraging translation equivariance constraints to improve temporal consistency and high-frequency detail preservation. Evaluations on the curated Flickr-Faces-HQ (FFHQ) subset (12,163 images) show that our approach achieves high fidelity and structural accuracy, while maintaining inference speed of 27.68 FPS. In perceptual studies on CelebV-Text video sequences, our results are consistently preferred over diffusion and GAN-based baselines for ocular consistency, temporal stability, and overall restoration quality.

</details>


### [58] [Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention for Hierarchical Feature Modulation toward Anatomy-Agnostic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20229)
*Mosharof Hossain,Md Rabiul Islam,Limon Halder,Erchin Serpedin,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA)机制，通过池化提取紧凑通道描述符并在共享空间中与语义提示融合，实现编码器-解码器网络中层级化的提示驱动通道调制。基于此构建PROMISE-Net（含CNN和Transformer两个变体），在四个医学影像分割基准上较基线U-Net和UNETR均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式分割方法通过晚期融合整合提示，缺乏在层级特征表示中进行提示驱动通道调制的显式机制，难以捕捉更深层的上下文和模态特定变化，限制了在低对比度、模糊边界和模态伪影场景下的分割性能。

Method: 提出PCCA机制：(1) 通过池化提取紧凑通道描述符；(2) 将其投影到共享空间；(3) 通过门控激励机制融合，计算提示感知的通道注意力权重；(4) 这些权重在多个网络阶段自适应重新校准特征响应。基于此构建PROMISE-Net，包含CNN变体（PROMISE-CNN）和Transformer变体（PROMISE-Txformer）。

Result: 在ISIC-Lesion、Kvasir-Polyp、CAMUS-Cardiac和Kvasir-Instrument基准上，PROMISE-CNN相对于基线U-Net的IoU增益分别为10.4%、8.7%、0.8%和3.4%；PROMISE-Txformer相对于基线UNETR的增益分别为7.6%、23.0%、2.1%和1.1%。

Conclusion: PCCA和PROMISE-Net在不同架构、成像模态和解剖目标上均展现出一致的性能改进，是一种可扩展、可泛化的提示感知层级特征调制框架，适用于医学图像分割任务。

Abstract: Anatomically plausible segmentation remains challenging because of low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and modality-specific artifacts. Interactive segmentation has emerged as a promising strategy to guide feature extraction and improve localization, particularly in structurally ambiguous regions. However, existing methods integrate prompts through late-stage fusion and lack explicit mechanisms for prompt-driven channel-wise modulation across hierarchical feature representations, limiting their ability to capture deeper contextual and modality-specific variations. To address these limitations, we introduce Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA), a novel modulation mechanism that enables deep, hierarchical integration of semantic prompts within encoder-decoder networks. PCCA extracts compact channel descriptors via pooling, projects them into a shared space, and fuses them through a gated excitation mechanism to compute prompt-aware channel attention weights. These weights adaptively recalibrate feature responses across multiple network stages, enabling prompt-conditioned, semantically enriched hierarchical representations. Building on this, we propose PROMISE-Net, instantiated in two network variants: a convolutional model (PROMISE-CNN) and a transformer-based model (PROMISE-Txformer). Across the ISIC-Lesion, Kvasir-Polyp, CAMUS-Cardiac, and Kvasir-Instrument benchmarks, integrating PCCA into PROMISE-CNN yielded relative IoU gains of 10.4%, 8.7%, 0.8%, and 3.4%, respectively, over the baseline U-Net, while PROMISE-Txformer achieved corresponding gains of 7.6%, 23.0%, 2.1%, and 1.1%, respectively, over the baseline UNETR. These results show consistent improvements across architectures, imaging modalities, and anatomical targets, establishing PCCA and PROMISE-Net as a scalable, generalizable framework for prompt-aware hierarchical feature modulation in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [59] [Ultra-High-Definition Restoration Transformers with Correlation Matching Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20263)
*Cong Wang,Liyan Wang,Jinshan Pan,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Jun Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UHDformer++，一个通用的基于Transformer的超高清图像复原框架。该框架在四个协调的学习空间中运作（高分辨率空间HR、低分辨率空间LR、超分辨率空间SR、低高融合重构空间LHFR），并通过FR-CMT和ACM两个模块桥接高低分辨率空间。相比最新方法，模型参数减少至少86%，在5个UHD复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清（UHD）图像复原任务繁多且计算成本高，现有方法通常针对单一任务设计且参数规模庞大，缺乏一个通用高效的框架来同时处理多种UHD复原任务。

Method: 提出四空间联合学习框架：HR空间提取多尺度高分辨率特征；LR空间学习紧凑代表性特征；SR空间将低分辨率特征上采样；LHFR空间进行最终融合与重建。引入FR-CMT模块（基于最大池化和均值池化融合特征选择高信息量通道替换低分辨率Transformer中信息量低的通道）和ACM模块（自适应调制多尺度高分辨率特征以传递任务相关信息至LR空间）。

Result: 在5个UHD复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上均获得显著性能提升，同时相较最新最先进方法模型参数至少减少86%。

Conclusion: UHDformer++证明了一个统一的Transformer框架能够有效且高效地处理多种UHD图像复原任务，大幅降低参数规模的同时提升性能，具有很好的泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose UHDformer++, a general Transformer-based framework to solve numerous Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) image restoration tasks. UHDformer++ operates across $4$ coordinated learning spaces: 1) a high-resolution space (HR) for multi-level feature extraction, 2) a low-resolution space (LR) for learning compact, representative features, 3) a super-resolution space (SR) for upsampling low-resolution features from SR, and 4) a low-high fusion and reconstruction space (LHFR) for final image restoration. Specifically, HR extracts multi-scale high-resolution features and fuses them with low-resolution cues to produce residual images, while LR distills complementary representations from HR to improve restoration quality. To supply LHFR with richer features, SR super-resolves LR outputs before fusion. We further introduce two modules to bridge the high- and low-resolution spaces. The Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation (FR-CMT) module selects the top $C/r~(C~\text{denotes the number of channels;~}r\geq1~\text{controls the squeezing level})$, from the fusion between max- and mean-pooled high-resolution features to replace less informative channels in the low-resolution Transformer. The Adaptive Channel Modulator (ACM) adaptively recalibrates multi-scale high-resolution features, ensuring that only task-relevant information propagates to LR. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UHDformer++ reduces model parameters by at least 86\% compared with recent state-of-the-art methods while achieving substantial performance gains across $5$ UHD restoration tasks, including low-light image enhancement, dehazing, deblurring, deraining, and desnowing. Code will be released at https://github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus.

</details>


### [60] [Towards Surgical World-Action Modeling: A Preliminary Joint Visual-Trajectory Forecasting for Surgical Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20284)
*Weiliang Huang,Huanrong Liu,Bob Zhang,Qi Dou,Zhen Chen,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合预测手术视觉状态和器械轨迹的世界-动作模型，通过分块自回归预测十五个未来步骤，在PSNR和ADE指标上均优于一次性直接预测，证明了联合视觉-运动预测的初步可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将未来手术场景生成和器械轨迹预测视为两个独立任务：仅场景模型无法在轨迹级别评估器械运动准确性，而仅轨迹模型无法捕捉器械运动带来的视觉变化，导致预测轨迹与未来场景演化之间的一致性未被关注。

Method: 将历史视频帧和器械轨迹编码为潜在表示，经时空编码器处理后，通过分离的视觉状态和轨迹预测头进行解码；基于该架构，采用分块自回归滚动预测方式，重复预测未来的十五个时间步骤。

Result: 分块策略在所有评估时域上均优于一次性直接预测：首个分段的PSNR从18.86 dB提升至23.11 dB，ADE从45.77像素降至22.22像素。

Conclusion: 联合视觉-运动预测的初步可行性已得到验证，但长时间预测中存在视觉逐渐退化和轨迹误差累积的问题，是未来手术世界-动作建模的重要挑战。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models to anticipate not only how instruments will move, but also how the operative visual state will evolve together with such motion. Existing approaches typically treat future scene generation and instrument trajectory prediction as two separate tasks. Scene-only models cannot directly evaluate the accuracy of future instrument motion at the trajectory level, while trajectory-only models fail to capture the visual consequences of instrument movement, leaving the consistency between predicted trajectories and future scene evolution unaddressed. Jointly forecasting both provides a more complete account of surgical action-scene dynamics by enabling explicit trajectory-level evaluation while simultaneously modeling the corresponding visual evolution. To bridge this gap, we present a preliminary joint visual-trajectory world-action model that simultaneously forecasts future visual states and instrument trajectories from historical surgical observations. Specifically, we encode historical video frames and tool trajectories into latent representations, which are processed by a temporal-spatial encoder and subsequently decoded through separate visual-state and trajectory prediction heads. Based on this preliminary architecture, a chunked autoregressive rollout is repeatedly applied to predict fifteen future steps. The chunked strategy consistently outperforms direct one-shot prediction across all evaluated horizons, improving first-segment PSNR from 18.86 to 23.11 dB and reducing ADE from 45.77 to 22.22 pixels. These results demonstrate the initial feasibility of joint visual-motion forecasting. However, we observe progressive visual degradation and accumulated trajectory errors over longer prediction horizons, which remain important challenges for future surgical world-action modeling.

</details>


### [61] [CalcSeg: Confidence-aware 3D Latent Context Curriculum Learning For Myocardial Scar Segmentation From Single-Stack LGE-CMRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20305)
*Nivetha Jayakumar,Hannah Kim,Amit R. Patel,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalcSeg是一个基于置信度感知的潜在上下文课程学习框架，用于从单堆栈2D LGE-CMR图像中进行心肌疤痕分割。它通过动态半监督课程学习策略（利用置信度感知评分函数）和潜在切片级自注意力机制（捕捉层间依赖以推断3D空间表示），在多中心临床数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单堆栈LGE-CMR心肌疤痕分割面临低组织对比度、弥漫性和小面积疤痕等挑战，且缺乏3D空间上下文，现有方法难以在这些临床困难场景下获得稳健的分割结果。

Method: 提出CalcSeg框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态半监督课程学习策略，通过置信度感知评分函数（结合分割误差、认知不确定性量化和疤痕负担估计）自动评估样本难度并渐进式训练；(2) 潜在切片级自注意力机制，从稀疏2D输入中捕捉层间依赖并推断3D空间表示。

Result: 在多中心临床LGE-CMR数据集上与现有疤痕分割网络对比，CalcSeg在所有评估指标上持续优于竞争方法，在临床挑战性病例上尤其取得了显著改善。

Conclusion: CalcSeg通过融合置信度感知课程学习与潜在3D上下文建模，有效解决了单堆栈LGE-CMR疤痕分割中的空间上下文不足和难样本学习问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Myocardial scar segmentation from single-stack late gadolinium-enhanced cardiac magnetic resonance (LGE-CMR) imaging has been a longstanding and clinically important challenge, particularly in the presence of low tissue contrast, diffuse, and small scar regions. These challenges are further intensified by the limited availability of 3D spatial context. This paper presents CalcSeg, a Confidence-aware latent context curriculum learning framework that leverages fused 3D feature representations from single-stack 2D LGE-CMR images for robust scar segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic semi-supervised curriculum learning strategy that progressively expands training from easier to more challenging scar cases using a learned confidence-aware scoring function. Such a function integrates errors in the predicted scar maps with quantified epistemic uncertainty and scar burden estimation to automatically assess sample difficulty without requiring manual labels. To compensate for the limited spatial context in single-stack acquisitions, we then develop a latent slice-wise self-attention to capture inter-slice dependencies and infer 3D spatial representations from sparse 2D inputs. We evaluate CalcSeg on multi-center clinical LGE-CMR datasets and benchmark against existing scar segmentation networks. Experimental results show that CalcSeg consistently outperforms all competing methods, particularly with substantial improvements on clinically challenging cases. Our code is released on Github.

</details>


### [62] [DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20308)
*Yufei Liu,Xixi Wang,Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Kaitong Cai,Chengkai Jin,Chunxiao Liu,Jianbo Liu,Siyuan Huang,Xingang Pan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHand提出了一种离线片段级框架，将视频扩散模型重新用作确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播从干净潜在空间中提取特征，用于从第一人称视频中恢复被遮挡和脱离视野的手部3D轨迹，在五个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧和窗口时序回归器在手部短暂离开画面时失败，而视频扩散模型作为像素渲染器依赖重量级随机多步采样，难以高效恢复第一人称视频中被严重遮挡和经常脱离视野的手部度量3D轨迹。

Method: 将VDM重新用作确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播在干净潜在空间暴露超出当前观察的场景内容（含遮挡及脱离视野的手部）。核心组件包括确定性干净潜在编码器和双向时空解码器；另提出基于射线的相机求解器以支持无测试时相机内参的配置。

Result: 在五个自我中心基准上取得新SOTA，ARCTIC上MPJPE-p降低30%，HOT3D上降低40%；当评估包含脱离视野手部时，增益达46%-61%。

Conclusion: DreamHand为从日常人类视频到机器人操作数据提供了一条可扩展的路径，能够在无需外部检测器和测试时相机内参的情况下恢复连续的双手轨迹及度量位置。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers scalable manipulation data for embodied AI, yet recovering metric 3D hand trajectories remains challenging due to severe object occlusion and frequent out-of-sight gaps. Existing single-frame and windowed temporal regressors fail when hand shortly leaves the frame, while recent video diffusion models (VDMs) rely on heavy, stochastic multi-step sampling as pixel-space renderers. We instead repurpose VDM into a deterministic geometry encoder. A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [63] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Inter-X++，一个大规模、高保真的人-人交互（HHI）基准数据集，包含11,388个交互序列和超过810万帧，配备丰富多模态标注（细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果顺序、关系人格、接触图和物理约束）。同时提出OpenHHI，一个统一的HHI表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建和语义理解，在生成与感知任务上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HHI数据集和建模方法存在低保真运动学、缺乏灵巧手部姿态、多模态标注严重不足等瓶颈；同时交互表示碎片化和评估协议不一致阻碍了公平严格的基准评测。亟需一个系统性的基准来解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 通过新型混合动作捕捉系统采集高保真交互序列；2) 提供包括层次化细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果交互顺序、主体关系与人格、顶点级接触图和物理正则约束在内的多维度标注；3) 构建覆盖生成与感知两大范式的四类下游任务统一测试平台；4) 标准化交互表示和评估协议；5) 提出OpenHHI统一表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建和语义理解。

Result: Inter-X++提供了11,388个高保真交互序列和超过810万帧数据，涵盖全身运动与精细手指关节。OpenHHI在生成和感知任务上均达到最先进性能（SOTA），证明统一表示能同时桥接交互理解与生成。

Conclusion: 统一的HHI表示能够同时促进交互的理解与生成，Inter-X++数据集及其标准化协议为HHI分析提供了全面可靠的基准，OpenHHI框架验证了统一表示方法在桥接感知与生成方面的有效性。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [64] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Swift-Image，一个紧凑的统一视觉生成模型，通过系统性的训练工程在有限计算预算下实现文本到图像生成、单图编辑和多图编辑任务。模型采用6B参数的单流DiT架构，结合渐进式训练管线、并行专家强化学习、多教师在线蒸馏以及提示增强器，并支持结构剪枝和少步蒸馏实现高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在受限计算预算下，通过系统性的训练工程方法，小巧的视觉生成模型能够达到多高的性能上限。

Method: 采用高效的6B单流DiT架构；渐进式训练流程从广泛语义覆盖逐步到高分辨率、高质量和统一生成-编辑监督；后训练阶段使用并行专家强化学习和多教师在线策略蒸馏；采用Prompt Enhancer解耦高层推理与像素渲染；通过结构剪枝和少步蒸馏获得3B及加速变体。

Result: Swift-Image以6B参数和243K GPU训练小时在开源模型中取得领先的聚合性能；压缩后的3B模型性能几乎无损失，少步蒸馏以显著更少的采样步骤进一步提升了聚合编辑性能。

Conclusion: 研究总结了架构、数据课程、后训练、提示增强和模型压缩方面的实用经验教训。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [65] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone是一个从无标定单目视频重建4D人体的框架，通过生成重建级多视角一致视频并将其提升为4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）。针对现有相机控制视频扩散模型在扩展至数十个目标视角时的一致性失败问题，提出了参考上下文打包（RCP）和目标上下文路由（TCR）两种互补设计，分别解决参考上下文O(N)增长和组间信息隔离两个瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制的视频扩散模型能合成合理的多视角视频，但当扩展到4DGS重建所需的数十个目标视角时无法保持一致性，其根源是有限注意力上下文问题：目标视角超出单个DiT前向传播能力需分组处理，导致参考侧O(N)增长的上下文削弱跨视角外观指导，目标侧分组间无法直接交换信息引发全局结构漂移。

Method: 提出4DAnyone框架：1）参考上下文打包（RCP）：将不断增长的参考视角压缩为固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，实现O(1)参考上下文复杂度；2）目标上下文路由（TCR）：在去噪过程中旋转目标视角分组，高噪声步共享上下文、低噪声步稳定细节；3）构建了MVGameHuman数据集（基于游戏引擎）并与光场和野外视频数据集联合训练。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans数据集上的实验表明，4DAnyone在生成视角视频质量和下游4DGS重建方面均优于先前方法，并具有稳健的野外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone通过解决扩散模型中的有界注意力上下文瓶颈，成功实现了从单目视频到4D人体重建的高质量多视角一致视频生成，为4D人体重建提供了一种可行且更具泛化性的方案。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [66] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WithEveryone框架，用于生成包含多达十个参考身份的群体图像。通过注入地址化令牌、预测结构化身份-布局计划，并提出Layout-Grounded ID Loss和ID Representation Forcing，在身份相似度、副本伪影和身份覆盖率等指标上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当场景中必须包含多个指定人物时，身份保持的图像生成变得不可靠。模型不仅需要保留每个身份，还要将每个参考人物绑定到不同的个体和位置，同时训练时的身份损失需要在多个噪声预测人脸间建立对应关系，这是现有方法的难点。

Method: WithEveryone包含三个关键组件：将每个选定的身份注入为地址化令牌；预测结构化的身份-布局计划并将其渲染为视觉条件；核心目标是Layout-Grounded ID Loss，利用标注的人脸区域直接监督目标身份，避免不稳定的基于嵌入的人脸匹配；此外ID Representation Forcing在图像合成前为每个身份训练一个预测。

Result: 在身份不相交的基准上，WithEveryone实现了最高的目标上下文身份相似度，将人脸相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升至0.499，同时将复制粘贴伪影从0.169降至0.055。此外覆盖了97.3%的请求身份，重复率仅为2.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式的身份-布局锚定能够使身份保持生成扩展到更大的群体，而无需依赖直接的参考人脸复制。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>

### [67] [A Virtual Member of a Community of Practice for the Society of Petroleum Engineers: From Prototype to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19199)
*John Boden,Joshua Eckroth,Dayne Freitag,Skyler Gipson,Johnathan Keefe,Karen Myers,Eric Schoen,Pedro Sequeira,Reid Smith,Michael Wessel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为ATHENA的虚拟助手，用于支持石油天然气行业实践社区的知识捕获、检索和传播。论文描述了从第一版原型到增强版的技术演进（包括多文档检索、答案验证支持和主动式知识传播），评估显示增强版在知识密集型井规划任务上优于最先进的RAG基线系统，并已集成到SPE研究门户中部署使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对石油天然气行业实践社区中知识管理效率低下的问题，开发一个虚拟助手以支持知识密集型任务的完成，同时解决第一版原型在检索和传播方面的不足。

Method: 开发并迭代升级ATHENA虚拟助手，第一版由75名SPE专业人士评估，识别改进方向后，在三个技术领域进行增强：多文档检索、答案验证支持以及更聚焦的主动式知识传播。

Result: 第一版评估显示ATHENA显著提升了专家在真实井规划任务上的生产力和绩效均衡性；增强版在知识密集型井规划任务上提供了优于最先进基线的支持；ATHENA已成功集成到SPE研究门户并部署于协会会员使用。

Conclusion: ATHENA作为支持实践社区知识管理的虚拟助手，通过持续的技术迭代显著提升了知识检索与传播的效率，并已完成从原型到正式部署应用的转化，证明了其在专业社区中的实用价值。

Abstract: We describe the evolution of a virtual assistant, called ATHENA, designed to support the capture, retrieval, and dissemination of knowledge for members of a Community of Practice (CoP) related to the Oil and Gas sector. An evaluation of a first prototype involving 75 professionals from the Society of Petroleum Engineering (SPE) showed that ATHENA dramatically improved both their productivity and performance equality on a set of realistic well-planning tasks compare to their use of a state-of-the-art RAG baseline system. However, the evaluation also identified areas for improvement. This paper describes technical advances to our first prototype in the areas of multi-document retrieval, support for answer validation, and more focused proactive dissemination. Evaluation results show that this enhanced version of ATHENA provides better support for completing knowledge-intensive tasks related to well planning than does a state-of-the-art baseline. ATHENA has been integrated into the SPE Research Portal and is being deployed for use by the society's membership.

</details>


### [68] [Transformer Models for Text Summarization: A Comparative Study of BART, BERT, and RoBERTa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19200)
*Daisy Aptovska,Vinayak Elangovan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对现代文本摘要技术进行了聚焦性综述，重点介绍了基于Transformer的模型和大型语言模型（特别是BERT、RoBERTa和BART），分析了它们的架构、预训练策略以及各自在抽取式和生成式摘要任务中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本摘要技术在NLP领域发展迅速，但现有综述缺乏对基于Transformer和大型语言模型的现代摘要技术的系统梳理，需要有针对性地总结这一领域的最新进展。

Method: 采用综述方法，按输入类型（单文档/多文档）和输出类型（抽取式、生成式、混合式）对ATS方法进行分类，并深入分析BERT、RoBERTa和BART的架构、预训练策略及其在抽取式和生成式摘要任务中的适用性。

Result: 系统地梳理了现代文本摘要技术，特别是Transformer模型和LLM在摘要任务中的应用，比较了BERT、RoBERTa和BART在不同摘要任务类型中的表现与适配性。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的模型和LLM已成为现代文本摘要的主流技术方向，BERT和RoBERTa更适用于抽取式摘要，而BART等生成式模型在生成式摘要中表现突出。

Abstract: Text summarization refers to the task of condensing a document into a shorter version while preserving its key information. Automatic text summarization (ATS), driven by advancements in natural language processing (NLP), has developed rapidly in recent years. ATS methods are commonly categorized by input type (such as single-document or multi-document summarization) and by output type (extractive, abstractive, and hybrid). This article presents a focused review of modern summarization techniques with an emphasis on transformer based models and large language models (LLMs), specifically BERT, RoBERTa and BART. It examines their architectures, pretraining strategies, and their suitability for extractive and abstractive summarization tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Automatic bioinformatic software named entity recognition from literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19201)
*Hao Xuan,Rithvij Pasupuleti,Ben Liu,Haishuo Sun,Jun Zhang,Zijun Yao,Cuncong Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNAIL是一个混合命名实体识别框架，用于从生物医学文本中自动识别生物信息学软件和数据库名称，结合词法与语义建模策略，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法（包括bioNerDS2和ChatGPT等大语言模型），并可用于大规模文献分析和工具使用趋势研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学软件和数据库在科学文献中的提及往往不一致且难以大规模系统性识别，缺乏全面且最新的生物信息学资源目录阻碍了自动化生物医学知识提取和简化数据分析的进程。

Method: SNAIL采用混合命名实体识别框架，整合了互补的词法建模（捕捉SW/DB名称的拼写模式和上下文线索）和语义建模（利用SciBERT等基于Transformer的语言模型生成的上下文嵌入，并结合显式token掩蔽策略增强实体聚焦表示）。训练语料通过混合管道自动构建，整合了引文提示提取与大语言模型辅助蒸馏。

Result: 在两个独立基准数据集和真实研究文章上的评估表明，SNAIL大幅优于现有方法，包括领域特定方法bioNerDS2以及通用大语言模型ChatGPT、Gemini、Grok和Claude。应用于大规模文献分析还揭示了不同生物信息学子领域在期刊层面的偏好差异。

Conclusion: SNAIL为在科学文本中识别生物信息学资源提供了准确且可扩展的解决方案，并能够实现工具使用和研究趋势的系统性元分析。

Abstract: Bioinformatics software and databases are essential components of modern life science research, yet their mentions in the scientific literature are often inconsistent and difficult to systematically identify at scale. The lack of a comprehensive and up-to-date catalog of bioinformatics resources hinders efforts toward automated biomedical knowledge extraction and streamlined data analysis. Here we present SNAIL, a hybrid named entity recognition framework designed to automatically identify bioinformatics software and database (SW/DB) names from biomedical texts. SNAIL integrates complementary lexical and semantic modeling strategies. The lexical component captures orthographic patterns and contextual cues characteristic of SW/DB names, while the semantic component leverages contextual embeddings generated by transformer-based language models such as SciBERT, combined with an explicit token-masking strategy to enhance entity-focused representations. A large training corpus was constructed automatically through a hybrid pipeline that integrates citation-hinted extraction with large language model-assisted distillation. Evaluation on two independent benchmark datasets and real-world research articles demonstrates that SNAIL substantially outperforms existing approaches, including domain-specific methods such as bioNerDS2 and general-purpose large language models such as ChatGPT, Gemini, Grok and Claude. Applying SNAIL to large-scale literature analysis further reveals distinct journal-level preferences across bioinformatics subfields. These results demonstrate that SNAIL provides an accurate and scalable solution for identifying bioinformatics resources in scientific texts and enables systematic meta-analysis of tool usage and research trends.

</details>


### [70] [Asymmetric Attention Heads: Structured Head-Wise Context Allocation for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19203)
*Zimu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称注意力头（AAH），一种按头分配上下文长度的框架，将上下文长度视为每个头（或每组头）的显式分配变量，通过特征统计分组、层次化组织，同时保持标准MHA输出接口。实验表明在4096 token设置下，多种AAH局部分配变体实现了优于纯全注意力的验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力（MHA）为每个注意力头赋予相同的完整因果上下文范围，忽略了不同头在上下文中扮演不同角色的事实——有些头依赖于局部词汇/句法上下文，而另一些头则需要长距离关系（实体交互、话语链接、状态变化）。

Method: AAH框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 利用特征统计对注意力头进行分组；(2) 对组进行层次化组织；(3) 为各组分配因果局部窗口，同时保留标准平铺MHA输出接口。通过Attention Coverage Ratio (ACR)作为窗口路由诊断指标。

Result: 在4096 token seed-0实验中，多种AAH式局部分配变体达到比纯全注意力更低的验证损失。短预算消融实验显示，稳定的局部分配和头-窗口分配结构是重要的，但固定/局部控制变体也能与自适应层次方法竞争。

Conclusion: AAH可被理解为一种结构化的按头上下文分配机制，兼顾质量和可分析性，并非所有上下文分配变体都优于全注意力，但局部分配策略具有竞争力，且在头-窗口分配结构上存在可探索的设计空间。

Abstract: Standard multi-head attention (MHA) gives every head the same full causal context span, although heads can serve different contextual roles. Some heads may rely mainly on nearby lexical or syntactic context, while others may depend on longer-range relations such as entity interactions, discourse links, or state changes. We present Asymmetric Attention Heads (AAH), a head-wise context- allocation framework that treats context length as an explicit per-head or per-group allocation variable. AAH groups heads using feature-derived statistics, organizes these groups hierarchically, and assigns causal local windows while preserving the standard flat MHA output interface. In 4096- token seed-0 experiments, several AAH-style local-allocation variants achieve lower validation loss than pure full attention. Short-budget ablations show that stable local allocation and head-window assignment structure matter, while fixed/local controls can be competitive with adaptive hierarchy. We interpret AAH as a structured head-wise context-allocation mechanism for quality and analysis, with Attention Coverage Ratio (ACR) reported as a selected-window routing diagnostic

</details>


### [71] [Hallucination as a Feature, not a Defect: Evaluating a multi-agent architecture to transform speculative language-model outputs into testable scientific hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19206)
*Nicolas Rodriguez-Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Rust的多智能体编排系统，利用"叙事白日梦"与"执行控制"的对比作为功能类比，通过高熵生成器与基于网络检索的评估器之间的"认识论摩擦"循环，结合低熵语义瓶颈，来生成多样且可评估的科研假设。实验表明该系统在强约束场景下具有优势，但未表现出对简单自我反思方法的全面超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代大规模语言模型被对齐以抑制幻觉、优先事实检索，这限制了其组合创造力，可能导致语义过度拟合和多样性坍缩，从而限制推测性的研发活动。作者希望探索如何在受控条件下释放LLM的推测性生成能力。

Method: 构建一个基于Rust的多智能体编排系统，包含：(1) 高熵生成智能体（叙事白日梦模式）；(2) 基于网络检索的评估智能体（执行控制模式）；(3) 低熵语义瓶颈以减少噪声和重复。两者之间形成"认识论摩擦"循环，并进行了消融实验对比直接提示、自我反思、去语义过滤、去搜索接地、去横向透镜等条件。

Result: 系统在物理和社会科学领域生成了多样且有可行性评级的假设。直接提示在大多数指标上表现最弱，但完整系统并未普遍优于简单的自我反思方法。每种架构在原创性、可行性、多样性和经验接地性之间呈现不同权衡；完整系统仅在假设须满足强物理、经验或制度约束时展示主要优势。

Conclusion: 幻觉本身并非孤立有用，推测性生成只有在架构约束、经验接地和显式评估的共同限定下才具有价值。研究为LLM驱动的创造性科研假设生成提供了一个受控的架构范式。

Abstract: Contemporary Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly aligned to suppress hallucinations, prioritizing factual retrieval over combinatorial creativity. While crucial for mitigating misinformation, this alignment may also restrict speculative Research and Development (R&D) by encouraging what this work operationally treats as semantic overfitting and diversity collapse. In this paper, we propose a Rust-based multi-agent orchestration that uses the contrast between narrative daydreaming and executive control as a functional analogy, not as a neurocognitive claim. The system instigates an Epistemological Friction loop between a high-entropy generating agent and a web-grounded evaluating agent, mediated by a low-entropy semantic bottleneck intended to reduce noise and repetition. Initial experiments generated diverse, viability-rated hypotheses across physical and social-science domains. We additionally report an exploratory paired baseline and ablation study comparing the full system against direct prompting, self-reflection, removal of the semantic filter, removal of search grounding, and removal of lateral lenses. The results place direct prompting among the weakest conditions across most observed metrics, but they do not show a general superiority of the full system over simple self-reflection. Instead, they suggest that each architecture shifts the balance between originality, feasibility, diversity, and empirical grounding in different ways, and that the full system provides its main advantages when hypotheses must survive strong physical, empirical, or institutional constraints. These findings do not show that hallucination is useful in isolation; they suggest that speculative generation gains value only when constrained by architecture, empirical grounding, and explicit evaluation.

</details>


### [72] [Compliance, Capability, and Conflict: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs under System Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19207)
*Juan Yeo,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VSysBench基准，用于评测多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在系统消息约束下的遵循能力与基础能力保持情况，发现系统消息会显著降低基础任务准确率，开放权重模型在用户冲突下遵循能力崩溃，视觉相关约束对所有模型最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么仅评测文本约束，要么将约束嵌入用户轮次，缺乏对多模态场景下系统消息遵循度的评测，也未考察遵循约束是否以牺牲基础视觉-语言能力为代价。

Method: 基于MMVet-v2构建VSysBench基准，将约束分为5大类22个子类，涵盖文本指令到完全视觉驱动的约束，并为每个约束配对一个错位的对抗性对照项以压力测试指令层级。通过联合满意率（JSR）和跨约束敏感性（CCS）两个指标对响应进行约束遵循度和答案正确性的联合评分。

Result: 在16个MLLM上的实验表明：系统消息会显著侵蚀基础任务准确率；开放权重模型在用户冲突下遵循能力崩溃，而顶尖专有模型保持稳定；视觉驱动约束对所有模型都是最难处理的类别。

Conclusion: VSysBench揭示了MLLM在系统消息遵循方面存在系统性缺陷，特别是视觉驱动约束处理能力不足，以及开放权重模型在面对用户指令冲突时的脆弱性，为后续模型改进提供了明确的评测方向。

Abstract: Production deployments of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) increasingly rely on system messages to govern model behavior. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate constraints in text only or embed them into the user turn, leaving system-message adherence in multimodal contexts largely unmeasured; they also leave open whether compliance comes at the cost of foundational vision-language capabilities. We introduce VSysBench, a benchmark built on MMVet-v2 that organizes constraints into 5 main categories and 22 sub-categories, ranging from textual directives in visual contexts to fully vision-grounded ones, each paired with a misaligned counterpart that stress-tests the instructional hierarchy. VSysBench scores each response jointly along two axes, constraint compliance and answer correctness, via the Joint Satisfaction Rate (JSR) and Cross-Constraint Sensitivity (CCS). Across 16 MLLMs, we find that imposing system messages substantially erodes base task accuracy, that compliance collapses under user conflict for open-weight models while remaining stable for top proprietary ones, and that vision-grounded constraints are the hardest category for every model.

</details>


### [73] [When Irrelevant Text Matters: Affine Margin Shifts in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19208)
*Yinfeng Wang,Zhiyuan Yao,Zheren Fu,Lei Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中任务无关文本上下文对视觉判断任务的影响，通过二元视觉判断框架中的受控干预实验，发现无关文本会一致性地使模型预测产生偏差，且这种偏差遵循可估计的仿射变换规律，而非随机噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs频繁接触辅助文本上下文，但其对视觉基础任务的影响尚未被充分探索，需要对任务无关上下文的干扰效应进行系统性研究。

Method: 构建二元视觉判断框架，保持提示结构不变而改变辅助输入，利用候选之间对数概率差值定义决策边际，并分析上下文条件边际与非条件边际之间的仿射变换关系。

Result: 发现上下文条件边际与非条件边际之间存在一致的仿射变换规律，说明无关上下文是模型偏好的可估计扭曲而非随机噪声；仿射参数可解释为视觉承诺保持度和方向性答案偏差两种指标。

Conclusion: 研究提供了边际级别的诊断视角来理解MLLMs中无关上下文的影响，为未来噪声上下文鲁棒性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are frequently exposed to auxiliary textual context, the impact of which on visually grounded tasks remains underexplored.
